===== M0013 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 790-935
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
790 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
791 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
792 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
793 |     // that we know to be safe
794 |     switch (n.getType()) {
795 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
796 |       case Token.CAST:
797 |       case Token.AND:
798 |       case Token.BLOCK:
799 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
800 |       case Token.HOOK:
801 |       case Token.IF:
802 |       case Token.IN:
803 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
804 |       case Token.NUMBER:
805 |       case Token.OR:
806 |       case Token.THIS:
807 |       case Token.TRUE:
808 |       case Token.FALSE:
809 |       case Token.NULL:
810 |       case Token.STRING:
811 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
812 |       case Token.SWITCH:
813 |       case Token.TRY:
814 |       case Token.EMPTY:
815 |         break;
816 | 
817 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
818 |       case Token.THROW:
819 |         return true;
820 | 
821 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
822 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
823 |           return true;
824 |         }
825 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
826 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
827 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
828 |             return true;
829 |           }
830 |         }
831 |         return false;
832 | 
833 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
834 |       case Token.REGEXP:
835 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
836 |           return true;
837 |         }
838 |         break;
839 | 
840 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
841 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
842 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
843 |           return true;
844 |         }
845 |         break;
846 | 
847 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
848 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
849 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
850 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
851 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
852 | 
853 |       case Token.NEW:
854 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
855 |           return true;
856 |         }
857 | 
858 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
859 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
860 |           // side-effects
861 |           break;
862 |         }
863 |         return true;
864 | 
865 |       case Token.CALL:
866 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
867 |         // side effect property set.
868 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
869 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
870 |           // side-effects
871 |           break;
872 |         }
873 |         return true;
874 | 
875 |       default:
876 |         if (isSimpleOperator(n)) {
877 |           break;
878 |         }
879 | 
880 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
881 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
882 |           if (assignTarget.isName()) {
883 |             return true;
884 |           }
885 | 
886 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
887 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
888 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
889 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
890 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
891 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
892 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
893 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
894 |             return true;
895 |           }
896 | 
897 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
898 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
899 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
900 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
901 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
902 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
903 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
904 |               return false;
905 |             }
906 | 
907 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
908 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
909 |             // other objects.
910 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
911 |             // side-effect.
912 |             while (isGet(current)) {
913 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
914 |             }
915 | 
916 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
917 |           } else {
918 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
919 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
920 |             // preserve existing behavior.
921 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
922 |           }
923 |         }
924 | 
925 |         return true;
926 |     }
927 | 
928 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
929 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
930 |         return true;
931 |       }
932 |     }
933 | 
934 |     return false;
935 |   }

===== M0017 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 159-205
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
159 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
160 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
161 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
162 |       case Token.BLOCK:
163 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
164 |       case Token.TRY:
165 |         return true;
166 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
167 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
168 |         // bleeds into the local scope and parameters are assigned
169 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
170 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
171 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
172 |         // control is going into the function's body, represented by the second
173 |         // child.
174 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
175 |       case Token.WHILE:
176 |       case Token.DO:
177 |       case Token.IF:
178 |         // These control structures are represented by a node that holds the
179 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
180 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.FOR:
183 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structures in that
184 |         // it has an initialization and an increment statement. Those
185 |         // two statements have corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
186 |         // The FOR node only represents the condition check for each iteration.
187 |         // That way the following:
188 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
189 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
190 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
191 |           // TODO(user): Investigate how we should handle the case where
192 |           // we have a very complex expression inside the FOR-IN header.
193 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
194 |         } else {
195 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
196 |         }
197 |       case Token.SWITCH:
198 |       case Token.CASE:
199 |       case Token.CATCH:
200 |       case Token.WITH:
201 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
202 |       default:
203 |         return false;
204 |     }
205 |   }

===== M0019 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.NumberNode.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 168-181
Documentation:

Method code:
168 |     @Override
169 |     boolean isEquivalentTo(
170 |         Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recur, boolean shallow) {
171 |       boolean equiv = super.isEquivalentTo(node, compareJsType, recur, shallow);
172 |       if (equiv) {
173 |         double thisValue = getDouble();
174 |         double thatValue = ((NumberNode) node).getDouble();
175 |         if (thisValue == thatValue) {
176 |           // detect the difference between 0.0 and -0.0.
177 |           return (thisValue != 0.0) || (1 / thisValue == 1 / thatValue);
178 |         }
179 |       }
180 |       return false;
181 |     }

809 |     boolean valid = false;

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1425-1461
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1425 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1426 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1427 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1428 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1429 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1430 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1431 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1432 |           continue;
1433 |         }
1434 |       }
1435 | 
1436 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1437 | 
1438 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1439 |       if (n == null) {
1440 |         continue;
1441 |       }
1442 | 
1443 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1444 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1445 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1446 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1447 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1448 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1449 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1450 | 
1451 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1452 | 
1453 |         externs.add(input);
1454 |         staleInputs = true;
1455 |       }
1456 |     }
1457 | 
1458 |     if (staleInputs) {
1459 |       repartitionInputs();
1460 |     }
1461 |   }

===== M0053 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 157-179
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
157 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
158 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
159 | 
160 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
161 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
162 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
163 | 
164 |       // ignore cast nodes.
165 |       while (first.isCast()) {
166 |         first = first.getFirstChild();
167 |       }
168 | 
169 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
170 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
171 |       }
172 | 
173 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
174 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
175 |       if (first.isName() &&
176 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
177 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
178 |       }
179 |     }

===== M0054 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 103-756
Documentation:

Method code:
103 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
104 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
105 |       return;
106 |     }
107 | 
108 |     int type = n.getType();
109 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
110 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
111 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
112 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
113 | 
114 |     // Handle all binary operators
115 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(
117 |           childCount == 2,
118 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
119 |           opstr, childCount);
120 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
121 | 
122 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
123 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
124 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
125 | 
126 |       // Handle associativity.
127 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
128 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
129 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
130 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
131 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
132 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
133 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
134 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
135 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
136 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
137 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
138 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
139 |       } else {
140 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
141 |       }
142 |       return;
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
146 | 
147 |     switch (type) {
148 |       case Token.TRY: {
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
150 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
152 | 
153 |         add("try");
154 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
155 | 
156 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
157 |         // isn't a catch block
158 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
159 |         if (catchblock != null) {
160 |           add(catchblock);
161 |         }
162 | 
163 |         if (childCount == 3) {
164 |           add("finally");
165 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
166 |         }
167 |         break;
168 |       }
169 | 
170 |       case Token.CATCH:
171 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
172 |         add("catch(");
173 |         add(first);
174 |         add(")");
175 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
176 |         break;
177 | 
178 |       case Token.THROW:
179 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
180 |         add("throw");
181 |         add(first);
182 | 
183 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
184 |         // parse this.
185 |         cc.endStatement(true);
186 |         break;
187 | 
188 |       case Token.RETURN:
189 |         add("return");
190 |         if (childCount == 1) {
191 |           add(first);
192 |         } else {
193 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
194 |         }
195 |         cc.endStatement();
196 |         break;
197 | 
198 |       case Token.VAR:
199 |         if (first != null) {
200 |           add("var ");
201 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
202 |         }
203 |         break;
204 | 
205 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
206 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
207 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.NAME:
211 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
212 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
213 |         } else {
214 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
215 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
216 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
217 |           if (first.isComma()) {
218 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
219 |           } else {
220 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
221 |             // precedence.
222 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
223 |           }
224 |         }
225 |         break;
226 | 
227 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
228 |         add("[");
229 |         addArrayList(first);
230 |         add("]");
231 |         break;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
234 |         add("(");
235 |         addList(first);
236 |         add(")");
237 |         break;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.COMMA:
240 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
241 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
242 |         break;
243 | 
244 |       case Token.NUMBER:
245 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
246 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
247 |         break;
248 | 
249 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
250 |       case Token.VOID:
251 |       case Token.NOT:
252 |       case Token.BITNOT:
253 |       case Token.POS: {
254 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
255 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
256 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
257 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
258 |         break;
259 |       }
260 | 
261 |       case Token.NEG: {
262 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
263 | 
264 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
265 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
266 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
267 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
268 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
269 |         } else {
270 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
271 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         break;
275 |       }
276 | 
277 |       case Token.HOOK: {
278 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
279 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
280 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
281 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
282 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
283 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
284 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
285 |         break;
286 |       }
287 | 
288 |       case Token.REGEXP:
289 |         if (!first.isString() ||
290 |             !last.isString()) {
291 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
292 |         }
293 | 
294 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
295 | 
296 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
297 |         if (childCount == 2) {
298 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
299 |         } else {
300 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
301 |           add(regexp);
302 |         }
303 |         break;
304 | 
305 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
306 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
307 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
308 |         }
309 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
310 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
311 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
312 |           add("(");
313 |         }
314 | 
315 |         add("function");
316 |         add(first);
317 | 
318 |         add(first.getNext());
319 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
320 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
321 | 
322 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
323 |           add(")");
324 |         }
325 |         break;
326 | 
327 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
328 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
329 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
330 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
331 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
332 | 
333 |         // Get methods are unnamed
334 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
335 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
336 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
337 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
338 |           add("get ");
339 |         } else {
340 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
341 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
342 |           add("set ");
343 |         }
344 | 
345 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
346 |         String name = n.getString();
347 |         Node fn = first;
348 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
349 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
350 | 
351 |         // Add the property name.
352 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
353 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
354 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
355 |             // Unicode escaped.
356 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
357 |           add(name);
358 |         } else {
359 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
360 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
361 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
362 |             cc.addNumber(d);
363 |           } else {
364 |             addJsString(n);
365 |           }
366 |         }
367 | 
368 |         add(parameters);
369 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
370 |         break;
371 | 
372 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
373 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
374 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
375 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
376 |         }
377 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
378 |         if (preserveBlock) {
379 |           cc.beginBlock();
380 |         }
381 | 
382 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
383 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
384 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
385 |                 !preserveBlock &&
386 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
387 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
388 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
389 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
390 | 
391 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
392 |           if (c.isVar()) {
393 |             cc.endStatement();
394 |           }
395 | 
396 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
397 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
398 |           }
399 | 
400 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
401 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
402 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
403 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
404 |           }
405 |         }
406 |         if (preserveBlock) {
407 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
408 |         }
409 |         break;
410 |       }
411 | 
412 |       case Token.FOR:
413 |         if (childCount == 4) {
414 |           add("for(");
415 |           if (first.isVar()) {
416 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
417 |           } else {
418 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
419 |           }
420 |           add(";");
421 |           add(first.getNext());
422 |           add(";");
423 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
424 |           add(")");
425 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
426 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
427 |         } else {
428 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
429 |           add("for(");
430 |           add(first);
431 |           add("in");
432 |           add(first.getNext());
433 |           add(")");
434 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
435 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
436 |         }
437 |         break;
438 | 
439 |       case Token.DO:
440 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
441 |         add("do");
442 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
443 |         add("while(");
444 |         add(last);
445 |         add(")");
446 |         cc.endStatement();
447 |         break;
448 | 
449 |       case Token.WHILE:
450 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
451 |         add("while(");
452 |         add(first);
453 |         add(")");
454 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
455 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
456 |         break;
457 | 
458 |       case Token.EMPTY:
459 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
460 |         break;
461 | 
462 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
463 |         Preconditions.checkState(
464 |             childCount == 2,
465 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
466 |         Preconditions.checkState(
467 |             last.isString(),
468 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
469 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
470 |         if (needsParens) {
471 |           add("(");
472 |         }
473 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
474 |         if (needsParens) {
475 |           add(")");
476 |         }
477 |         add(".");
478 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
479 |         break;
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       case Token.GETELEM:
483 |         Preconditions.checkState(
484 |             childCount == 2,
485 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
486 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
487 |         add("[");
488 |         add(first.getNext());
489 |         add("]");
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.WITH:
493 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
494 |         add("with(");
495 |         add(first);
496 |         add(")");
497 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
498 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
499 |         break;
500 | 
501 |       case Token.INC:
502 |       case Token.DEC: {
503 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
504 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
505 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
506 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
507 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
508 |         if (postProp != 0) {
509 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
510 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
511 |         } else {
512 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
513 |           add(first);
514 |         }
515 |         break;
516 |       }
517 | 
518 |       case Token.CALL:
519 |         // We have two special cases here:
520 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
521 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
522 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
523 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
524 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
525 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
526 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
527 |         // that must be preserved.
528 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
529 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
530 |           add("(0,");
531 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
532 |           add(")");
533 |         } else {
534 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
535 |         }
536 |         add("(");
537 |         addList(first.getNext());
538 |         add(")");
539 |         break;
540 | 
541 |       case Token.IF:
542 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
543 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
544 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
545 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
546 |           cc.beginBlock();
547 |         }
548 | 
549 |         add("if(");
550 |         add(first);
551 |         add(")");
552 | 
553 |         if (hasElse) {
554 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
555 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
556 |           add("else");
557 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
558 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
559 |         } else {
560 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
561 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
562 |         }
563 | 
564 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
565 |           cc.endBlock();
566 |         }
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.NULL:
570 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
571 |         cc.addConstant("null");
572 |         break;
573 | 
574 |       case Token.THIS:
575 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
576 |         add("this");
577 |         break;
578 | 
579 |       case Token.FALSE:
580 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
581 |         cc.addConstant("false");
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.TRUE:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         cc.addConstant("true");
587 |         break;
588 | 
589 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
590 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
591 |         add("continue");
592 |         if (childCount == 1) {
593 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
594 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
595 |           }
596 |           add(" ");
597 |           add(first);
598 |         }
599 |         cc.endStatement();
600 |         break;
601 | 
602 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
603 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
604 |         add("debugger");
605 |         cc.endStatement();
606 |         break;
607 | 
608 |       case Token.BREAK:
609 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
610 |         add("break");
611 |         if (childCount == 1) {
612 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
613 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
614 |           }
615 |           add(" ");
616 |           add(first);
617 |         }
618 |         cc.endStatement();
619 |         break;
620 | 
621 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
622 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
623 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
624 |         cc.endStatement();
625 |         break;
626 | 
627 |       case Token.NEW:
628 |         add("new ");
629 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
630 | 
631 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
632 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
633 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
634 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
635 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
636 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
637 |         }
638 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
639 | 
640 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
641 |         Node next = first.getNext();
642 |         if (next != null) {
643 |           add("(");
644 |           addList(next);
645 |           add(")");
646 |         }
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
650 |         Preconditions.checkState(
651 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
652 |         addJsString(n);
653 |         break;
654 | 
655 |       case Token.STRING:
656 |         Preconditions.checkState(
657 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
658 |         addJsString(n);
659 |         break;
660 | 
661 |       case Token.DELPROP:
662 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
663 |         add("delete ");
664 |         add(first);
665 |         break;
666 | 
667 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
668 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
669 |         if (needsParens) {
670 |           add("(");
671 |         }
672 |         add("{");
673 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
674 |           if (c != first) {
675 |             cc.listSeparator();
676 |           }
677 | 
678 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
679 |             add(c);
680 |           } else {
681 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
682 |             String key = c.getString();
683 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
684 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
685 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
686 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
687 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
688 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
689 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
690 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
691 |               add(key);
692 |             } else {
693 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
694 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
695 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
696 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
697 |               } else {
698 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
699 |               }
700 |             }
701 |             add(":");
702 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
703 |           }
704 |         }
705 |         add("}");
706 |         if (needsParens) {
707 |           add(")");
708 |         }
709 |         break;
710 |       }
711 | 
712 |       case Token.SWITCH:
713 |         add("switch(");
714 |         add(first);
715 |         add(")");
716 |         cc.beginBlock();
717 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
718 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
719 |         break;
720 | 
721 |       case Token.CASE:
722 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
723 |         add("case ");
724 |         add(first);
725 |         addCaseBody(last);
726 |         break;
727 | 
728 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
729 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
730 |         add("default");
731 |         addCaseBody(first);
732 |         break;
733 | 
734 |       case Token.LABEL:
735 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
736 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
737 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
738 |         }
739 |         add(first);
740 |         add(":");
741 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
742 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
743 |         break;
744 | 
745 |       case Token.CAST:
746 |         add("(");
747 |         add(first);
748 |         add(")");
749 |         break;
750 | 
751 |       default:
752 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
753 |     }
754 | 
755 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
756 |   }

/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.
   *
   * For the purposes of this function, we define a MaybeFunctionType as any
   * type in the sub-lattice
   * { x | LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE <= x <= GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE }
   * This definition excludes bottom types like NoType and NoObjectType.
   *
   * This definition is somewhat arbitrary and axiomatic, but this is the
   * definition that makes the most sense for the most callers.
   */
Method code:
350 |   public FunctionType toMaybeFunctionType() {
351 |     return null;
352 |   }

===== M0077 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.IR.mayBeStatementNoReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/IR.java
Original range: 503-532
Documentation:
/**
   * It isn't possible to always determine if a detached node is a expression,
   * so make a best guess.
   */
Method code:
503 |   private static boolean mayBeStatementNoReturn(Node n) {
504 |     switch (n.getType()) {
505 |       case Token.EMPTY:
506 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
507 |         // EMPTY and FUNCTION are used both in expression and statement
508 |         // contexts
509 |         return true;
510 | 
511 |       case Token.BLOCK:
512 |       case Token.BREAK:
513 |       case Token.CONST:
514 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
515 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
516 |       case Token.DO:
517 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
518 |       case Token.FOR:
519 |       case Token.IF:
520 |       case Token.LABEL:
521 |       case Token.SWITCH:
522 |       case Token.THROW:
523 |       case Token.TRY:
524 |       case Token.VAR:
525 |       case Token.WHILE:
526 |       case Token.WITH:
527 |         return true;
528 | 
529 |       default:
530 |         return false;
531 |     }
532 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.toSource(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1809-1813
Documentation:
/**
   * Generates JavaScript source code for an AST, doesn't generate source
   * map info.
   */
Method code:
1809 |   @Override

===== M0092 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1237-1299
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1237 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1238 |     // unknown
1239 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1240 |       return true;
1241 |     }
1242 |     // all type
1243 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1244 |       return true;
1245 |     }
1246 |     // equality
1247 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1248 |       return true;
1249 |     }
1250 |     // unions
1251 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1252 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1253 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1254 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1255 |           return true;
1256 |         }
1257 |       }
1258 |       return false;
1259 |     }
1260 | 
1261 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType() && thatType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1262 |       JSType rawThisType = thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType();
1263 |       JSType rawThatType = thatType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType();
1264 |       if (!rawThisType.isSubtype(rawThatType)) {
1265 |         return false;
1266 |       }
1267 | 
1268 |       TemplateTypeMap thisTypeParams = thisType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1269 |       TemplateTypeMap thatTypeParams = thatType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1270 | 
1271 |       if (thisTypeParams.checkEquivalenceHelper(
1272 |           thatTypeParams, EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT)) {
1273 |         return true;
1274 |       } else if (isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType)) {
1275 |         // Only array and object qualify and they are compatible if their
1276 |         // elmenet types are related.
1277 |         TemplateType key = thisType.registry.getObjectElementKey();
1278 |         JSType thisElement = thisTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1279 |         JSType thatElement = thatTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1280 | 
1281 |         return thisElement.isSubtype(thatElement)
1282 |             || thatElement.isSubtype(thisElement);
1283 |       } else {
1284 |         return false;
1285 |       }
1286 |     }
1287 | 
1288 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1289 |       return thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1290 |               thatType);
1291 |     }
1292 | 
1293 |     // proxy types
1294 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1295 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1296 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1297 |     }
1298 |     return false;
1299 |   }

===== M0093 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateStatement(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 93-153
Documentation:

Method code:
93 |   public void validateStatement(Node n) {
94 |     switch (n.getType()) {
95 |       case Token.LABEL:
96 |         validateLabel(n);
97 |         return;
98 |       case Token.BLOCK:
99 |         validateBlock(n);
100 |         return;
101 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
102 |         validateFunctionStatement(n);
103 |         return;
104 |       case Token.WITH:
105 |         validateWith(n);
106 |         return;
107 |       case Token.FOR:
108 |         validateFor(n);
109 |         return;
110 |       case Token.WHILE:
111 |         validateWhile(n);
112 |         return;
113 |       case Token.DO:
114 |         validateDo(n);
115 |         return;
116 |       case Token.SWITCH:
117 |         validateSwitch(n);
118 |         return;
119 |       case Token.IF:
120 |         validateIf(n);
121 |         return;
122 |       case Token.VAR:
123 |         validateVar(n);
124 |         return;
125 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
126 |         validateExprStmt(n);
127 |         return;
128 |       case Token.RETURN:
129 |         validateReturn(n);
130 |         return;
131 |       case Token.THROW:
132 |         validateThrow(n);
133 |         return;
134 |       case Token.TRY:
135 |         validateTry(n);
136 |         return;
137 |       case Token.BREAK:
138 |         validateBreak(n);
139 |         return;
140 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
141 |         validateContinue(n);
142 |         return;
143 |       case Token.EMPTY:
144 |         validateChildless(n);
145 |         return;
146 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
147 |         validateChildless(n);
148 |         return;
149 |       default:
150 |         violation("Expected statement but was "
151 |             + Token.name(n.getType()) + ".", n);
152 |     }
153 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1442-1461
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1442 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1443 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false, false)) {
1444 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1445 |     }
1446 | 
1447 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1448 |     Node n, n2;
1449 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1450 |          res == null && n != null;
1451 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1452 |       if (node2 == null) {
1453 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1454 |       }
1455 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1456 |       if (res != null) {
1457 |         return res;
1458 |       }
1459 |     }
1460 |     return res;
1461 |   }

===== M0116 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 484-523
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
485 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
486 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
487 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
488 |         parent.isTry() &&
489 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
490 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
491 |       return;
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
495 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
496 | 
497 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
498 |     // function (unless it is called)
499 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
500 |       child = child.getNext();
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     if (child != null) {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
505 |     } else {
506 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     // Synthetic blocks
510 |     if (parent != null) {
511 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
512 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
513 |         case Token.CASE:
514 |         case Token.TRY:
515 |           break;
516 |         default:
517 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
518 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
519 |           }
520 |           break;
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |   }

741 |     @Override
742 |     Node processFunctionCall(FunctionCall callNode) {
743 |       Node node = newNode(transformTokenType(callNode.getType()),
744 |                            transform(callNode.getTarget()));
745 |       for (AstNode child : callNode.getArguments()) {
746 |         node.addChildToBack(transform(child));
747 |       }
748 | 
749 |       node.setLineno(node.getFirstChild().getLineno());
750 |       node.setCharno(node.getFirstChild().getCharno());
751 |       maybeSetLengthFrom(node, callNode);
752 |       return node;
753 |     }

1487 |     switch (n.getType()){

1500 |         return true;
1501 |     }
1502 |     return false;

===== M0141 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneNode()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1697-1711
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node, specifically excluding its children.
   */
Method code:
1697 |   public Node cloneNode() {
1698 |     Node result;
1699 |     try {
1700 |       result = (Node) super.clone();
1701 |       // PropListItem lists are immutable and can be shared so there is no
1702 |       // need to clone them here.
1703 |       result.next = null;
1704 |       result.first = null;
1705 |       result.last = null;
1706 |       result.parent = null;
1707 |     } catch (CloneNotSupportedException e) {
1708 |       throw new RuntimeException(e.getMessage());
1709 |     }
1710 |     return result;
1711 |   }

Method code:
67 |         @Override
68 |         public void visit(AbstractCompiler compiler, Node root) {
69 |           // Computes the control flow graph.
70 |           ControlFlowAnalysis cfa =
71 |               new ControlFlowAnalysis(compiler, false, false);
72 |           cfa.process(null, root);
73 |           ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg = cfa.getCfg();
74 |           new GraphReachability<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>(cfg)
75 |               .compute(cfg.getEntry().getValue());
76 |           if (root.isFunction()) {
77 |             root = root.getLastChild();
78 |           }
79 |           do {
80 |             codeChanged = false;
81 |             NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, new EliminationPass(cfg));
82 |           } while (codeChanged);
83 |         }

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();

===== M0173 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1512-1563
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recur Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @param shallow If true, the method doesn't recur into inner functions.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1512 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(
1513 |       Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recur, boolean shallow) {
1514 |     if (type != node.getType()
1515 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1516 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1517 |       return false;
1518 |     }
1519 | 
1520 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1521 |       return false;
1522 |     }
1523 | 
1524 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1525 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1526 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1527 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1528 |         return false;
1529 |       }
1530 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1531 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1532 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1533 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1534 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1535 |           return false;
1536 |         }
1537 |       }
1538 | 
1539 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1540 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1541 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1542 |         return false;
1543 |       }
1544 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1545 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1546 |         return false;
1547 |       }
1548 |     }
1549 | 
1550 |     if (recur) {
1551 |       Node n, n2;
1552 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1553 |            n != null;
1554 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1555 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(
1556 |             n2, compareJsType, !(shallow && n.isFunction()), shallow)) {
1557 |           return false;
1558 |         }
1559 |       }
1560 |     }
1561 | 
1562 |     return true;
1563 |   }

===== M0186 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.TemplateTypeMap.checkEquivalenceHelper(TemplateTypeMap,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/TemplateTypeMap.java
Original range: 166-183
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if this map and the specified map have equivalent template
   * types.
   */
Method code:
166 |   public boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
167 |       TemplateTypeMap that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
168 |     int thisNumKeys = templateKeys.size();
169 |     int thatNumKeys = that.getTemplateKeys().size();
170 | 
171 |     for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(thisNumKeys, thatNumKeys); i++) {
172 |       JSType thisTemplateType = getTemplateType(templateKeys.get(i));
173 |       JSType thatTemplateType = that.getTemplateType(
174 |           that.getTemplateKeys().get(i));
175 |       if (!thisTemplateType.checkEquivalenceHelper(
176 |           thatTemplateType, eqMethod)) {
177 |         return false;
178 |       }
179 |     }
180 | 
181 |     return thisNumKeys == thatNumKeys ||
182 |         eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT;
183 |   }

===== M0210 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleSwitch(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 434-449
Documentation:

Method code:
434 |   private void handleSwitch(Node node) {
435 |     // Transfer to the first non-DEFAULT CASE. if there are none, transfer
436 |     // to the DEFAULT or the EMPTY node.
437 |     Node next = getNextSiblingOfType(
438 |         node.getFirstChild().getNext(), Token.CASE, Token.EMPTY);
439 |     if (next != null) { // Has at least one CASE or EMPTY
440 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, next);
441 |     } else { // Has no CASE but possibly a DEFAULT
442 |       if (node.getFirstChild().getNext() != null) {
443 |         createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, node.getFirstChild().getNext());
444 |       } else { // No CASE, no DEFAULT
445 |         createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
446 |       }
447 |     }
448 |     connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
449 |   }

===== M0216 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-536
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     int type = n.getType();
512 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
513 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
514 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
515 |     }
516 | 
517 |     curNode = n;
518 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) {
519 |       return;
520 |     }
521 | 
522 |     if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
523 |       traverseFunction(n, parent);
524 |     } else {
525 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
526 |         // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
527 |         // would no longer point to the true next
528 |         Node next = child.getNext();
529 |         traverseBranch(child, n);
530 |         child = next;
531 |       }
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     curNode = n;
535 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
536 |   }

===== M0218 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 796-810
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthrough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
796 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
797 |     switch (n.getType()) {
798 |       case Token.DO:
799 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
800 |       case Token.FOR:
801 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
802 |           return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
803 |         }
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
805 |       case Token.LABEL:
806 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
807 |       default:
808 |         return n;
809 |     }
810 |   }

===== M0220 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.mayThrowException(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 910-931
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
910 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
911 |     switch (n.getType()) {
912 |       case Token.CALL:
913 |       case Token.GETPROP:
914 |       case Token.GETELEM:
915 |       case Token.THROW:
916 |       case Token.NEW:
917 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
918 |       case Token.INC:
919 |       case Token.DEC:
920 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
921 |         return true;
922 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
923 |         return false;
924 |     }
925 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
926 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
927 |         return true;
928 |       }
929 |     }
930 |     return false;
931 |   }

/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1716 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1717 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1718 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1719 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1720 |       n2clone.parent = result;

1728 |     }
1729 |     return result;
1730 |   }

===== M0258 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.removeChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1798-1851
Documentation:
/** Safely remove children while maintaining a valid node structure. */
Method code:
1798 |   static void removeChild(Node parent, Node node) {
1799 |     if (isTryFinallyNode(parent, node)) {
1800 |       if (NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(getCatchBlock(parent))) {
1801 |         // A finally can only be removed if there is a catch.
1802 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1803 |       } else {
1804 |         // Otherwise, only its children can be removed.
1805 |         node.detachChildren();
1806 |       }
1807 |     } else if (node.isCatch()) {
1808 |       // The CATCH can can only be removed if there is a finally clause.
1809 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent().getParent();
1810 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1811 |       node.detachFromParent();
1812 |     } else if (isTryCatchNodeContainer(node)) {
1813 |       // The container node itself can't be removed, but the contained CATCH
1814 |       // can if there is a 'finally' clause
1815 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent();
1816 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1817 |       node.detachChildren();
1818 |     } else if (node.isBlock()) {
1819 |       // Simply empty the block.  This maintains source location and
1820 |       // "synthetic"-ness.
1821 |       node.detachChildren();
1822 |     } else if (isStatementBlock(parent)
1823 |         || isSwitchCase(node)) {
1824 |       // A statement in a block can simply be removed.
1825 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1826 |     } else if (parent.isVar()) {
1827 |       if (parent.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1828 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1829 |       } else {
1830 |         // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1831 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1832 |         // This would leave an empty VAR, remove the VAR itself.
1833 |         removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1834 |       }
1835 |     } else if (parent.isLabel()
1836 |         && node == parent.getLastChild()) {
1837 |       // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1838 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1839 |       // A LABEL without children can not be referred to, remove it.
1840 |       removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1841 |     } else if (parent.isFor()
1842 |         && parent.getChildCount() == 4) {
1843 |       // Only Token.FOR can have an Token.EMPTY other control structure
1844 |       // need something for the condition. Others need to be replaced
1845 |       // or the structure removed.
1846 |       parent.replaceChild(node, IR.empty());
1847 |     } else {
1848 |       throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid attempt to remove node: " +
1849 |           node.toString() + " of " + parent.toString());
1850 |     }
1851 |   }

Method code:
464 |     @Override
465 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
466 |       if (lineBreak) {
467 |         if (sawFunction) {
468 |           startNewLine();
469 |           sawFunction = false;
470 |         }
471 |       }
472 | 
473 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
474 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
475 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
476 |       int len = code.length();
477 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
478 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
479 |         if (ch == ';') {
480 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
481 |         }
482 |       }
483 |       maybeCutLine();
484 |     }

/** Set the prototype without doing any sanity checks. */
Method code:
444 |   private boolean setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
445 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
446 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
447 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
448 | 
449 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
450 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
451 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
452 | 
453 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
454 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
455 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
456 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
457 |     }
458 | 
459 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
460 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
461 |       if (superClass != null) {
462 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
463 |       }
464 | 
465 |       if (isInterface()) {
466 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
467 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
468 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
469 |           }
470 |         }
471 |       }
472 |     }
473 |

475 |       clearCachedValues();
476 |     }
477 | 
478 |     return true;
479 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 120-285
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.BITAND:
125 |       case Token.BITOR:
126 |       case Token.BITXOR:
127 |       case Token.COMMA:
128 |       case Token.DIV:
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.GE:
131 |       case Token.GT:
132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

===== M0310 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 112-155
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
112 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
113 |                                   String sourceString,
114 |                                   Config config,
115 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
116 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
117 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
118 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
119 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
120 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
121 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
122 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
123 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
124 | 
125 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
126 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
127 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
128 | 
129 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
130 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
131 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
132 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
133 | 
134 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
135 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
136 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
137 | 
138 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
139 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
140 |     try {
141 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
142 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
143 |       logger.info(
144 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
145 |     } finally {
146 |       Context.exit();
147 |     }
148 |     Node root = null;
149 |     if (astRoot != null) {
150 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
151 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
152 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
153 |     }
154 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
155 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.LinkedDirectedGraph.isConnectedInDirection(N,Predicate<E>,N)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/LinkedDirectedGraph.java
Original range: 203-215
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {
208 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2 &&
209 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {
210 |         return true;
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     return false;
215 |   }

===== M0333 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1168-1188
Documentation:

Method code:
1168 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1169 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1170 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1171 |       return s;
1172 |     }
1173 | 
1174 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1175 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1176 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1177 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1178 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1179 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1180 |       // crazy.
1181 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1182 |         sb.append(c);
1183 |       } else {
1184 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1185 |       }
1186 |     }
1187 |     return sb.toString();
1188 |   }

515 | 
516 |     boolean seenCatchOrFinally = false;
517 | 
518 |     // Validate catch
519 |     Node catches = n.getChildAtIndex(1);
520 |     validateNodeType(Token.BLOCK, catches);
521 |     validateMaximumChildCount(catches, 1);
522 |     if (catches.hasChildren()) {
523 |       validateCatch(catches.getFirstChild());
524 |       seenCatchOrFinally = true;
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     // Validate finally
528 |     if (n.getChildCount() == 3) {
529 |       validateBlock(n.getLastChild());
530 |       seenCatchOrFinally = true;
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     if (!seenCatchOrFinally) {
534 |       violation("Missing catch or finally for try statement.", n);
535 |     }
536 |   }

===== M0347 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleContinue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 601-637
Documentation:

Method code:
601 |   private void handleContinue(Node node) {
602 |     String label = null;
603 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
604 |       label = node.getFirstChild().getString();
605 |     }
606 |     Node cur;
607 |     Node previous = null;
608 |     Node lastJump;
609 | 
610 |     // Similar to handBreak's logic with a few minor variation.
611 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
612 |     for (cur = node, lastJump = node;
613 |         !isContinueTarget(cur, parent, label);
614 |         cur = parent, parent = parent.getParent()) {
615 |       if (cur.isTry() && NodeUtil.hasFinally(cur)
616 |           && cur.getLastChild() != previous) {
617 |         if (lastJump == node) {
618 |           createEdge(lastJump, Branch.UNCOND, cur.getLastChild());
619 |         } else {
620 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump, computeFallThrough(cur.getLastChild()));
621 |         }
622 |         lastJump = cur;
623 |       }
624 |       Preconditions.checkState(parent != null, "Cannot find continue target.");
625 |       previous = cur;
626 |     }
627 |     Node iter = cur;
628 |     if (cur.getChildCount() == 4) {
629 |       iter = cur.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
630 |     }
631 | 
632 |     if (lastJump == node) {
633 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, iter);
634 |     } else {
635 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, iter);
636 |     }
637 |   }
