===== M0693 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.verifyScopeChanges(Map<Node, Node>,Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3314-3344
Documentation:
/** Checks that the scope roots marked as changed have indeed changed */
Method code:
3314 |   public static void verifyScopeChanges(Map<Node, Node> map,
3315 |       Node main, boolean verifyUnchangedNodes) {
3316 |     // If verifyUnchangedNodes is false, we are comparing the initial AST to the
3317 |     // final AST. Don't check unmarked nodes b/c they may have been changed by
3318 |     // non-loopable passes.
3319 |     // If verifyUnchangedNodes is true, we are comparing the ASTs before & after
3320 |     // a pass. Check all scope roots.
3321 |     final Map<Node, Node> mtoc = map;
3322 |     final boolean checkUnchanged = verifyUnchangedNodes;
3323 |     Node clone = mtoc.get(main);
3324 |     if (main.getChangeTime() > clone.getChangeTime()) {
3325 |       Preconditions.checkState(!main.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3326 |     } else if (checkUnchanged) {
3327 |       Preconditions.checkState(main.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3328 |     }
3329 |     visitPreOrder(main,
3330 |         new Visitor() {
3331 |           @Override
3332 |           public void visit(Node n) {
3333 |             if (n.isFunction() && mtoc.containsKey(n)) {
3334 |               Node clone = mtoc.get(n);
3335 |               if (n.getChangeTime() > clone.getChangeTime()) {
3336 |                 Preconditions.checkState(!n.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3337 |               } else if (checkUnchanged) {
3338 |                 Preconditions.checkState(n.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3339 |               }
3340 |             }
3341 |           }
3342 |         },
3343 |         Predicates.<Node>alwaysTrue());
3344 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.

1314 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1315 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);

Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the predicate is true for the node or any of its children.
   */
Method code:
2704 |   static boolean has(Node node,
2705 |                      Predicate<Node> pred,
2706 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2707 |     if (pred.apply(node)) {
2708 |       return true;
2709 |     }
2710 | 
2711 |     if (!traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2712 |       return false;
2713 |     }
2714 | 
2715 |     for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2716 |       if (has(c, pred, traverseChildrenPred)) {
2717 |         return true;
2718 |       }
2719 |     }
2720 | 
2721 |     return false;
2722 |   }

===== M0731 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silenceThreshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silenceThreshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0735 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.getNonEmptyChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1194-1205
Documentation:
/**
   * @param maxCount The maximum number of children to look for.
   * @return The number of children of this node that are non empty up to
   * maxCount.
   */
Method code:
1194 |   private static int getNonEmptyChildCount(Node n, int maxCount) {
1195 |     int i = 0;
1196 |     Node c = n.getFirstChild();
1197 |     for (; c != null && i < maxCount; c = c.getNext()) {
1198 |       if (c.isBlock()) {
1199 |         i += getNonEmptyChildCount(c, maxCount - i);
1200 |       } else if (!c.isEmpty()) {
1201 |         i++;
1202 |       }
1203 |     }
1204 |     return i;
1205 |   }

===== M0739 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 209-237
Documentation:

Method code:
209 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
210 |     maybeEndStatement();
211 | 
212 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
213 |     char prev = getLastChar();
214 | 
215 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
216 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
217 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
218 |       append(" ");
219 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
220 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
221 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
222 |       append(" ");
223 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
224 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
225 |       append(" ");
226 |     }
227 | 
228 |     // Allow formatting around the operator.
229 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
230 | 
231 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
232 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
233 |     // because it looks strange.
234 |     if (binOp) {
235 |       maybeCutLine();
236 |     }
237 |   }

Original range: 505-521
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
505 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
506 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
507 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
508 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
509 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
510 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
511 |         String inputName = input.getName();
512 | 
513 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
514 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
515 |         // will get an error down the line.
516 |         inputs.add(input);
517 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
518 |       }
519 |     }
520 |     return inputs;
521 |   }

/**
   * Check if label is actually referencing the target control structure. If
   * label is null, it always returns true.
   */
Method code:
894 |   private static boolean matchLabel(Node target, String label) {
895 |     if (label == null) {
896 |       return true;
897 |     }
898 |     while (target.isLabel()) {
899 |       if (target.getFirstChild().getString().equals(label)) {

===== M0749 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 755-837
Documentation:

Method code:
755 |     @Override
756 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
757 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
758 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
759 |       if (name == null) {
760 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
761 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
762 |           errorReporter.error(
763 |             "unnamed function statement",
764 |             sourceName,
765 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
766 | 
767 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
768 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
769 |         }
770 |         name = new Name();
771 |         name.setIdentifier("");
772 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
773 |       }
774 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
775 |       Node newName = transform(name);
776 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
777 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
778 |         // declaration.
779 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
780 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
781 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
782 |         // same line as the function keyword.
783 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
784 |             functionNode.getLp();
785 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
786 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
787 |       }
788 | 
789 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
790 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
791 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
792 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
793 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
794 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
795 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
796 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
797 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
798 |       if (fnName != null) {
799 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
800 |       } else {
801 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
802 |       }
803 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
804 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
805 | 
806 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
807 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
808 |         Node paramNode = transformParameter(param);
809 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
810 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
811 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
812 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
813 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
814 |         } else {
815 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
816 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
817 |           // (an error has already been reported).
818 |           Preconditions.checkState(
819 |               config.isIdeMode
820 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
821 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
822 |         }
823 |       }
824 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
825 | 
826 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
827 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
828 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
829 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
830 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
831 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
832 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
833 |       }
834 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
835 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
836 |      return node;
837 |     }
